UpdateData:\n    customer: str\n    owner_name: str\n    window_start: str\n    window_end: str\n    band: str\n    escalation_email: str\n    note: str\n\n\ndef load_update_data(path: str) -> UpdateData:\n    with open(path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        obj = json.load(f)\n\n    return UpdateData(\n        customer=obj[\"customer\"],\n        owner_name=obj[\"owner_name\"],\n )\n\n\ndef load_followups(path: str) -> List[str]:\n    items: List[Tuple[int, str]] = []\n    with open(path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n        for row in reader:\n            seq = int(row[\"sequence\"].strip())\n            item = row[\"item\"].strip()\n            items.append((seq, item))\n\n    items.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    # Render with numbering 1..N in the sorted order.\n    return [f\"{i}. {item}\" for i, (_seq, item) in enumerate(items, start=1)]\n\n\ndef qname(local: str) -> str:\n    return f\"{{{HWPX_NS}}}{local}\"\n\n\ndef local_name(tag: str) -> str:\n    if tag.startswith(\"{\"):\n        return tag.split(\"}\", 1)[1]\n    return tag\n\n\ndef paragraph_text(p: ET._Element) -> str:\n    # Concatenate all text runs (hp:t). In HWPX, visible paragraph text is usually stored here.\n    parts: List[str] = []\n    for t in p.findall(\".//hp:t\", namespaces={\"hp\": HWPX_NS}):\n        if t.text:\n            parts.append(t.text)\n    return \"\".join(parts)\n\n\ndef set_paragraph_text(p: ET._Element, new_text: str) -> None:\n    ts = p.findall(\".//hp:t\", namespaces={\"hp\": HWPX_NS})\n    if not ts:\n        # Seen variants: hp:lineSegArray (camel) and hp:linesegarray (lowercase) in fixtures.\n    for el in list(p.xpath('.//*[local-name()=\"lineSegArray\" or local-name()=\"linesegarray\" or local-name()=\"lineSeg\" or local-name()=\"lineseg\"]')):\n        parent = el.getparent()\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(el)\n\n\ndef extract_existing_values(section_root: ET._Element) -> Dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract old values from labeled paragraphs when present.\n\n    This helps us do global replacements so old values disappear everywhere.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    existing: Dict[str, str] = {}\n\n    for p in section_root.findall(\".//hp:p\", namespaces={\"hp\": HWPX_NS}):\n        txt = paragraph_text(p)\n        if not txt or APPENDIX_SENTENCE in txt:\n            continue\n\n        if txt.startswith(\"고객사:\"):\n            existing[\"customer\"] = txt.split(\":\", 1)[1].strip()\n        elif txt.startswith(\"현 담당자:\"):\n            existing[\"owner_name\"] = txt.split(\":\", 1)[1].strip()\n        elif txt.startswith(\"갱신 윈도우:\"):\n            existing[\"window\"] = txt.split(\":\", 1)[1].strip()\n        elif txt.startswith(\"가격 메모:\"):\n            existing[\"note\"] = txt.split(\":\", 1)[1].strip()\n\n    # Table-based fields or standalone values\n    # 가격 밴드 cell and escalation cell are often plain text paragraphs.\n    for p in section_root.findall(\".//hp:p\", namespaces={\"hp\": HWPX_NS}):\n        txt = paragraph_text(p).strip()\n        if not txt or APPENDIX_SENTENCE in txt:\n            continue\n        if re.fullmatch(r\"[A-Za-z]+-\\d+M\", txt):\n            existing.setdefault(\"band\", txt)\n        if re.fullmatch(r\"[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+\", txt):\n            # Best-effort; only stash if it looks like the known cell (avoid capturing random emails).\n            if \"escalation\" not in existing and \"@\" in txt:\n                existing[\"escalation_email\"] = txt\n\n    return existing\n\n\ndef apply_field_updates(section_root: ET._Element, update: UpdateData, existing: Dict[str, str]) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Update field values across all paragraphs.\n\n    Returns count of modified paragraphs.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    modified = 0\n\n    old_customer = existing.get(\"customer\")\n    old_owner = existing.get(\"owner_name\")\n    old_window = existing.get(\"window\")\n    old_band = existing.get(\"band\")\n    old_escalation = existing.get(\"escalation_email\")\n    old_note = existing.get(\"note\")\n\n    for p in section_root.findall(\".//hp:p\", namespaces={\"hp\": HWPX_NS}):\n        txt = paragraph_text(p)\n        if not txt or APPENDIX_SENTENCE in txt:\n            continue\n\n        new_txt = txt\n\n        # Preferred: reconstruct labeled lines to avoid partial replacements.\n        if txt.startswith(\"고객사:\"):\n            new_txt = f\"고객사: {update.customer}\"\n        elif txt.startswith(\"현 담당자:\"):\n            new_txt = f\"현 담당자: {update.owner_name}\"\n        elif txt.startswith(\"갱신 윈도우:\"):\n            new_txt = f\"갱신 윈도우: {update.window_start} ~ {update.window_end}\"\n        elif txt.startswith(\"갱신 윈도우 확인:\"):\n            new_txt = f\"갱신 윈도우 확인: {update.window_start} ~ {update.window_end}\"\n        elif old_band and txt.strip() == old_band:\n            new_txt = update.band\n        elif old_escalation and txt.strip() == old_escalation:\n            new_txt = update.escalation_email\n        elif txt.startswith(\"가격 메모:\"):\n            new_txt = f\"가격 메모: {update.note}\"\n\n        # Also do global replacement of old values if they appear elsewhere.\n        replacements = [\n            (old_customer, update.customer),\n            (old_owner, update.owner_name),\n            (old_window, f\"{update.window_start} ~ {update.window_end}\"),\n            (old_band, update.band),\n            (old_escalation, update.escalation_email),\n            (old_note, update.note),\n        ]\n        for old, new in replacements:\n            if old and old in new_txt:\n                new_txt = new_txt.replace(old, new)\n\n        if new_txt != txt:\n            set_paragraph_text(p, new_txt)\n            remove_layout_caches(p)\n            modified += 1\n\n    return modified\n\n\ndef update_followups(section_root: ET._Element, followups: List[str]) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Replace follow-up paragraphs in section1.\n\n    Returns number of modified paragraphs (added/removed/updated).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Locate existing follow-up lines: paragraphs whose text starts with \"<number>. \".\n    followup_ps: List[ET._Element] = []\n    for p in section_root.findall(\".//hp:p\", namespaces={\"hp\": HWPX_NS}):\n        txt = paragraph_text(p)\n        if not txt or APPENDIX_SENTENCE in txt:\n            continue\n        if re.match(r\"^\\d+\\.\\s+\", txt):\n            followup_ps.append(p)\n\n    if not followup_ps:\n        return 0\n\n    parent = followup_ps[0].getparent()\n    if parent is None:\n        return 0\n\n    # We'll treat the first contiguous block of numbered paragraphs as the follow-up list.\n    # Find the contiguous slice in the parent's children.\n    children = list(parent)\n    idxs = [children.index(p) for p in followup_ps if p in children]\n    idxs.sort()\n    start_idx = idxs[0]\n    end_idx = start_idx\n    for i in idxs[1:]:\n        if i == end_idx + 1:\n            end_idx = i\n        else:\n            break\n\n    existing_block = children[start_idx : end_idx + 1]\n    existing_block = [p for p in existing_block if local_name(p.tag) == \"p\"]\n\n    modified = 0\n\n    def max_para_id(root: ET._Element) -> int:\n        max_id = -1\n        for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", namespaces={\"hp\": HWPX_NS}):\n            pid = p.get(\"id\")\n            if pid is None:\n                continue\n            try:\n                max_id = max(max_id, int(pid))\n            except ValueError:\n                continue\n        return max_id\n\n    next_id = max_para_id(section_root) + 1\n\n    # Update common prefix\n    common = min(len(existing_block), len(followups))\n    for i in range(common):\n        p = existing_block[i]\n        old_txt = paragraph_text(p)\n        if old_txt != followups[i]:\n            set_paragraph_text(p, followups[i])\n            remove_layout_caches(p)\n            modified += 1\n\n    # Preferred: reconstruct labeled lines to avoid partial replacements.\n        if txt.startswith(\"고객사:\"):\n            new_txt = f\"고객사: {update.customer}\"\n        elif txt.startswith(\"현 담당자:\"):\n            new_txt = f\"현 담당자: {update.owner_name}\"\n        elif txt.startswith(\"갱신 윈도우:\"):\n            new_txt = f\"갱신 윈도우: {update.window_start} ~ {update.window_end}\"\n        elif txt.startswith(\"갱신 윈도우 확인:\"):\n            new_txt = f\"갱신 윈도우 확인: {update.window_start} ~ {update.window_end}\"\n        elif old_band and txt.strip() == old_band:\n            new_txt = update.band\n        elif old_escalation and txt.strip() == old_escalation:\n            new_txt = update.escalation_email\n        elif txt.startswith(\"가격 메모:\"):\n            new_txt = f\"가격 메모: {update.note}\"\n\n        # Locate existing follow-up lines: paragraphs whose text starts with \"<number>. \".\n    followup_ps: List[ET._Element] = []\n    for p in section_root.findall(\".//hp:p\", namespaces={\"hp\": HWPX_NS}):\n        txt = paragraph_text(p)\n        if not txt or APPENDIX_SENTENCE in txt:\n            continue\n        if re.match(r\"^\\d+\\.\\s+\", txt):\n            followup_ps.append(p)\n\n    if not followup_ps:\n        return 0\n\n    parent = followup_ps[0].getparent()\n    if parent is None:\n        return 0\n\n    #
